function renderNotifications() {
  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
  unreadCount = 0; updateNotifBadge(); saveNotifications();
  main.innerHTML = `
    <div class="section-head"><div><h2>Notifications</h2>
      <p>Live updates received since you logged in. ${currentUser.role === 'CUSTOMER'
        ? 'You are told when your ticket is assigned, escalated or its status changes.'
        : 'You are told about new tickets, escalations, fraud alerts, SLA warnings, flagged files' + (currentUser.role === 'ADMIN' ? ' and new SLA reports.' : '.')}</p></div>
      ${notifications.length ? '<button class="secondary" onclick="notifications=[];saveNotifications();renderNotifications()">Clear</button>' : ''}</div>
    ${notifications.length ? notifications.map(n => `
      <div class="notif ${esc(n.eventType)}">
        <div>
          <b>${esc(n.message)}</b>
          <div class="t">${esc(labelForEvent(n.eventType))} &middot; ${n.ticketId ? 'ticket #' + n.ticketId + ' ' : ''}${esc(n.title)} &middot; ${fmtDate(n.timestamp)}</div>
        </div>
        <button class="link-btn" onclick="${n.ticketId ? `renderView('ticket-detail', ${n.ticketId})` : `renderView('admin-reports')`}">Open</button>
      </div>`).join('') : '<p class="empty">Nothing yet. Keep this tab open and updates will appear here as they happen.</p>'}`;
}
function labelForEvent(t) {
  return ({ STATUS_CHANGED: 'Status changed', ASSIGNED: 'Assigned', NEW_TICKET: 'New ticket', ESCALATED: 'Escalated',
     FRAUD_ALERT: 'Fraud alert', SLA_REPORT_READY: 'SLA report', MALICIOUS_FILE: 'Malicious file',
     SLA_WARNING: 'SLA warning', SLA_BREACHED: 'SLA breached' })[t] || t;
}

function renderProfile() {
  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
  main.innerHTML = `
    <div class="section-head"><div><h2>My profile</h2></div></div>
    <div class="two-col">
      <div class="card">
        <h3>Details</h3>
        <div id="profile-msg"></div>
        <div class="avatar-box">
          <div id="profile-avatar">${avatarHtml(true)}</div>
          <div>
            <label>Profile picture</label>
            <input type="file" id="profile-pic-file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg" onchange="previewProfilePic(this)">
            <p class="drop-note">PNG or JPEG, up to ${MAX_UPLOAD_MB} MB.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="btn-row"><button id="pic-btn" onclick="doUploadProfilePic()">Upload picture</button></div>
        ${currentUser.profilePicPath && !avatarUrl && !getCachedAvatar() ? `<p class="hint" style="margin-top:10px">
          A picture is saved, but the server did not return it. Check that GET /api/users/me/profile-pic exists, or upload it again.</p>` : ''}
        <hr class="divider">
        <div class="field"><label>Full name</label><input id="profile-name" value="${esc(currentUser.fullName)}"></div>
        <div class="field"><label>Email</label><input value="${esc(currentUser.email)}" disabled></div>
        <button onclick="doUpdateProfile()">Save name</button>
      </div>
      <div class="card">
        <h3>Change password</h3>
        <div id="password-msg"></div>
        <div class="field"><label>Current password</label><input type="password" id="current-password"></div>
        <div class="field"><label>New password (min 8 characters)</label><input type="password" id="new-password" minlength="8"></div>
        <button onclick="doChangePassword()">Change password</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    ${currentUser.role === 'CUSTOMER' ? '<div class="card" id="kyc-card" style="margin-top:16px;max-width:520px"><h3>Identity verification</h3><p class="empty">Loading…</p></div>' : ''}`;
  if (currentUser.role === 'CUSTOMER') loadKycCard();
}
async function previewProfilePic(input) {
  const f = input.files[0];
  if (!f) return;
  const problem = validateProfilePic(f);
  if (problem) { toast(problem, true); input.value = ''; return; }
  try {
    const url = await imageToSmallDataUrl(f);
    document.getElementById('profile-avatar').innerHTML = `<img class="avatar big" src="${url}" alt="Preview">`;
  } catch (e) { toast(e.message, true); }
}
function validateProfilePic(f) {
  if (!['image/png', 'image/jpeg'].includes(f.type)) return 'Profile pictures must be PNG or JPEG.';
  if (f.size > MAX_UPLOAD_MB * 1024 * 1024) return 'That image is larger than ' + MAX_UPLOAD_MB + ' MB.';
  return null;
}
async function doUpdateProfile() {
  const fullName = document.getElementById('profile-name').value;
  try {
    currentUser = await api('/api/users/me', { method: 'PUT', body: JSON.stringify({ fullName }) });
    setNavIdentity();
    refreshNavAvatar();
    document.getElementById('profile-msg').innerHTML = '<div class="msg ok">Saved.</div>';
  } catch (err) { document.getElementById('profile-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`; }
}
async function doUploadProfilePic() {
  const input = document.getElementById('profile-pic-file');
  if (!input.files.length) { toast('Choose an image first', true); return; }
  const file = input.files[0];
  const problem = validateProfilePic(file);
  if (problem) { toast(problem, true); return; }
  const btn = document.getElementById('pic-btn');
  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Uploading…';
  try {
    const small = await imageToSmallDataUrl(file);
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('file', file);
    currentUser = await api('/api/users/me/profile-pic', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
    cacheAvatar(currentUser.profilePicPath, small);
    await loadAvatar();
    refreshNavAvatar();
    renderProfile();
    document.getElementById('profile-msg').innerHTML = '<div class="msg ok">Profile picture uploaded.</div>';
  } catch (err) {
    btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Upload picture';
    document.getElementById('profile-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`;
  }
}
async function doChangePassword() {
  const currentPassword = document.getElementById('current-password').value;
  const newPassword = document.getElementById('new-password').value;
  try {
    await api('/api/users/change-password', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ currentPassword, newPassword }) });
    document.getElementById('password-msg').innerHTML = '<div class="msg ok">Password changed.</div>';
    document.getElementById('current-password').value = ''; document.getElementById('new-password').value = '';
  } catch (err) { document.getElementById('password-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`; }
}

/* ============================================================
   NOTIFICATIONS (SSE via fetch — native EventSource can't send
   our Authorization header, so we read the stream manually)
   ============================================================ */
let sseReconnectDelay = 2000;
let sseReconnectTimer = null;

async function connectNotifications() {
  if (sseAbortController) sseAbortController.abort();
  if (sseReconnectTimer) { clearTimeout(sseReconnectTimer); sseReconnectTimer = null; }
  sseAbortController = new AbortController();
  const myController = sseAbortController;

  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/notifications/subscribe', {
      headers: { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token },
      signal: myController.signal,
    });
    if (!res.ok || !res.body) { scheduleReconnect(); return; }
    sseReconnectDelay = 2000;

    const reader = res.body.getReader();
    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    let buffer = '';

    while (true) {
      const { value, done } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });

      const chunks = buffer.split('\n\n');
      buffer = chunks.pop();

      for (const chunk of chunks) {
        const dataLine = chunk.split('\n').find(l => l.startsWith('data:'));
        if (!dataLine) continue;
        try { handleLiveEvent(JSON.parse(dataLine.slice(5).trim())); }
        catch (e) { /* ignore malformed event */ }
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.name === 'AbortError') return;
  }
  if (myController === sseAbortController) {
    scheduleReconnect();
  }
}

function handleLiveEvent(n) {
  if (n && n.kind === 'system-event') { addSystemEvent(n); return; }   // Kafka monitor events are not notifications
  notifications.unshift(n);
  notifications = notifications.slice(0, 50);
  if (currentView !== 'notifications') { unreadCount++; updateNotifBadge(); }
  saveNotifications();
  const bad = n.eventType === 'SLA_BREACHED' || n.eventType === 'SLA_WARNING';
  const bad2 = bad || n.eventType === 'MALICIOUS_FILE' || n.eventType === 'FRAUD_ALERT';
  toast(n.ticketId ? `#${n.ticketId} ${n.title}: ${n.message}` : `${n.title}: ${n.message}`, bad2);

  if (currentView === 'ticket-detail' && n.ticketId && currentParam === n.ticketId) renderView('ticket-detail', n.ticketId);
  else if (currentView === 'queue') runQueueSearch();
  else if (currentView === 'my-tickets') renderMyTickets();
  else if (currentView === 'admin-reports' && n.eventType === 'SLA_REPORT_READY') renderAdminReports();
  else if (currentView === 'admin-fraud' && n.eventType === 'FRAUD_ALERT') renderAdminFraud();
  else if (currentView === 'notifications') renderNotifications();
}

function scheduleReconnect() {
  if (sseReconnectTimer) clearTimeout(sseReconnectTimer);
  sseReconnectTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    if (token) connectNotifications();
  }, sseReconnectDelay);
  sseReconnectDelay = Math.min(sseReconnectDelay * 2, 30000);
}

/* ============================================================
   STARTUP
   ============================================================ */
if (getResetToken()) {
  token = null;
  localStorage.removeItem('bankdesk_token');
  showAuthTab('reset');
} else if (token) {
  bootApp();
}

<script>
/* ============================================================
   STATE
   ============================================================ */

let token = localStorage.getItem('bankdesk_token') || null;
let currentUser = null;
let categories = [];
let currentView = null;
let sseAbortController = null;
let currentParam = null;          // id of the ticket currently open (used by live updates)
let notifications = [];           // newest first, kept in this browser per user
let unreadCount = 0;
let avatarUrl = null;            // blob: URL of the profile picture served by GET /api/users/me/profile-pic
let systemEvents = [];            // Kafka events shown in the admin System events tab
let aiResults = {};               // last AI answers per ticket (so a live refresh does not wipe them)
let sessionNotice = '';           // one-off message shown on the login screen (e.g. after a password reset)

const isStaffRole = r => r === 'AGENT' || r === 'ADMIN';
const ALLOWED_UPLOAD_TYPES = ['image/png', 'image/jpeg', 'application/pdf'];
const MAX_UPLOAD_MB = 10;         // client-side guard only; the server decides what it really accepts

function esc(v) {
  return String(v ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
}

/* ============================================================
   API HELPER
   ============================================================ */
async function api(path, opts = {}) {
  const headers = { ...(opts.headers || {}) };
  const isForm = opts.body instanceof FormData;
  if (!isForm) headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
  if (token) headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ' + token;

  const res = await fetch(path, { ...opts, headers });
  const raw = await res.text();
  let data = raw;
  try { data = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null; } catch (e) { /* plain text response */ }

  if (res.status === 401 && token && !path.startsWith('/api/auth/')) {
    logout();
    document.getElementById('auth-msg').innerHTML = '<div class="msg error">Your session expired. Please log in again.</div>';
    throw new Error('Your session expired. Please log in again.');
  }
  if (!res.ok) {
    const msg = (data && typeof data === 'object' && data.message)
      ? data.message
      : (typeof data === 'string' ? data : res.statusText);
    throw new Error(msg);
  }
  return data;
}

async function downloadFile(path, suggestedName) {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(path, { headers: { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token } });
    if (!res.ok) {
      let message = 'Could not download file';
      try { const body = await res.json(); if (body && body.message) message = body.message; } catch (e) { /* not JSON */ }
      if (!isStaffRole(currentUser && currentUser.role) && /malicious|flagged/i.test(message)) {
        message = 'This file is not available for download.';
      }
      toast(message, true);
      return;
    }
    const blob = await res.blob();
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = suggestedName || 'download';
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    a.remove();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  } catch (err) { toast('Could not download file', true); }
}

function toast(message, isError) {
  const host = document.getElementById('toast-host');
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'toast' + (isError ? ' error' : '');
  el.textContent = message;
  host.appendChild(el);
  setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 4200);
}

/* ============================================================
   AUTH SCREEN
   ============================================================ */
function showAuthTab(which) {
  document.getElementById('tab-login').classList.toggle('active', which === 'login');
  document.getElementById('tab-register').classList.toggle('active', which === 'register');
  document.getElementById('login-form').classList.toggle('hidden', which !== 'login');
  document.getElementById('register-form').classList.toggle('hidden', which !== 'register');
  document.getElementById('resend-form').classList.toggle('hidden', which !== 'resend');
  document.getElementById('forgot-form').classList.toggle('hidden', which !== 'forgot');
  document.getElementById('reset-form').classList.toggle('hidden', which !== 'reset');
  document.querySelector('.auth-toggle').classList.toggle('hidden', which === 'reset');
  document.getElementById('auth-msg').innerHTML = '';
}

async function handleLogin(e) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const email = document.getElementById('login-email').value;
  const password = document.getElementById('login-password').value;
  try {
    const res = await api('/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }) });
    token = res.token;
    localStorage.setItem('bankdesk_token', token);
    await bootApp();
  } catch (err) {
    document.getElementById('auth-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`;
  }
}

// demo accounts: fills the login form and logs in
async function quickLogin(email) {
  showAuthTab('login');
  document.getElementById('login-email').value = email;
  document.getElementById('login-password').value = 'Password123!';
  await handleLogin({ preventDefault() {} });
}

async function handleRegister(e) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const fullName = document.getElementById('reg-name').value;
  const email = document.getElementById('reg-email').value;
  const password = document.getElementById('reg-password').value;
  try {
    await api('/api/auth/register', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ fullName, email, password }) });
    document.getElementById('auth-msg').innerHTML =
      `<div class="msg ok">Account created. Check your inbox for the verification link, then log in.</div>`;
    showAuthTab('login');
  } catch (err) {
    document.getElementById('auth-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`;
  }
}

async function handleResendVerification(e) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const email = document.getElementById('resend-email').value;
  try {
    const res = await api('/api/auth/resend-verification', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ email }) });
    document.getElementById('auth-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg ok">${typeof res === 'string' ? res : 'A new verification email has been sent.'}</div>`;
    showAuthTab('login');
  } catch (err) {
    document.getElementById('auth-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`;
  }
}

async function handleForgotPassword(e) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const email = document.getElementById('forgot-email').value;
  try {
    const res = await api('/api/auth/forget-password', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ email }) });
    document.getElementById('auth-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg ok">${typeof res === 'string' ? res : 'If an account exists, a reset link has been sent.'}</div>`;
    showAuthTab('login');
  } catch (err) {
    document.getElementById('auth-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`;
  }
}

function getResetToken() { return new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('resetToken'); }
function leaveResetPage() {
  window.history.replaceState({}, '', window.location.pathname);
  showAuthTab('login');
}
async function handleResetPassword(e) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const pw = document.getElementById('reset-new-password').value;
  const pw2 = document.getElementById('reset-new-password2').value;
  const box = document.getElementById('auth-msg');
  if (pw !== pw2) { box.innerHTML = '<div class="msg error">The two passwords do not match.</div>'; return; }
  try {
    await api('/api/auth/reset-password', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ token: getResetToken(), newPassword: pw }) });
    window.history.replaceState({}, '', window.location.pathname);
    showAuthTab('login');
    document.getElementById('auth-msg').innerHTML = '<div class="msg ok">Password reset. You can log in with your new password.</div>';
  } catch (err) {
    box.innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`;
  }
}

function logout() {
  if (sseAbortController) sseAbortController.abort();
  if (sseReconnectTimer) { clearTimeout(sseReconnectTimer); sseReconnectTimer = null; }
  token = null;
  currentUser = null;
  notifications = [];
  unreadCount = 0;
  if (avatarUrl) { URL.revokeObjectURL(avatarUrl); avatarUrl = null; }
  localStorage.removeItem('bankdesk_token');
  document.getElementById('app-screen').classList.add('hidden');
  document.getElementById('auth-screen').classList.remove('hidden');
}

/* ============================================================
   BOOT
   ============================================================ */
async function bootApp() {
  try {
    currentUser = await api('/api/users/me');
  } catch (err) {
    logout();
    return;
  }
  document.getElementById('auth-screen').classList.add('hidden');
  document.getElementById('app-screen').classList.remove('hidden');
  setNavIdentity();
  await loadAvatar();
  refreshNavAvatar();
  loadNotifications();

  try { categories = await api('/api/categories'); } catch (e) { categories = []; }

  buildNav();
  connectNotifications();
  let defaultView = currentUser.role === 'CUSTOMER' ? 'my-tickets' : 'queue';
  // coming back from the identity verification page (?kyc=done)
  if (new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('kyc') && currentUser.role === 'CUSTOMER') {
    window.history.replaceState({}, '', window.location.pathname);
    defaultView = 'profile';
    toast('Thanks! We are checking your verification.');
  }
  renderView(defaultView);
}

function avatarKey() { return 'bd_avatar_' + (currentUser ? currentUser.id : ''); }
function getCachedAvatar() {
  try {
    const c = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(avatarKey()) || 'null');
    return (c && currentUser.profilePicPath && c.path === currentUser.profilePicPath) ? c.dataUrl : null;
  } catch (e) { return null; }
}
function cacheAvatar(path, dataUrl) {
  try { localStorage.setItem(avatarKey(), JSON.stringify({ path, dataUrl })); } catch (e) { /* storage full or blocked */ }
}
function initials(name, email) {
  const src = (name || email || '?').trim();
  const parts = src.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  return ((parts[0] || '?')[0] + (parts.length > 1 ? parts[parts.length - 1][0] : '')).toUpperCase();
}
async function loadAvatar() {
  if (avatarUrl) { URL.revokeObjectURL(avatarUrl); avatarUrl = null; }
  if (!currentUser || !currentUser.profilePicPath) return;
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/users/me/profile-pic', { headers: { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token } });
    if (!res.ok) return;                       // falls back to the local copy or initials
    avatarUrl = URL.createObjectURL(await res.blob());
  } catch (e) { /* offline: fall back */ }
}
function avatarHtml(big) {
  const img = avatarUrl || getCachedAvatar();
  const cls = 'avatar' + (big ? ' big' : '');
  return img ? `<img class="${cls}" src="${img}" alt="Profile picture">`
             : `<div class="${cls}">${esc(initials(currentUser.fullName, currentUser.email))}</div>`;
}
function setNavIdentity() {
  const email = document.getElementById('nav-email');
  email.textContent = currentUser.email;
  email.title = currentUser.email;
  document.getElementById('nav-name').textContent = currentUser.fullName || currentUser.email;
  document.getElementById('nav-role').textContent = currentUser.role;
}
function refreshNavAvatar() {
  const el = document.getElementById('nav-avatar');
  if (el) el.innerHTML = avatarHtml(false);
}
function imageToSmallDataUrl(file) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Could not read the image'));
    reader.onload = () => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Could not read the image'));
      img.onload = () => {
        const size = 160;
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        canvas.width = size; canvas.height = size;
        const side = Math.min(img.width, img.height);
        canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(img, (img.width - side) / 2, (img.height - side) / 2, side, side, 0, 0, size, size);
        resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85));
      };
      img.src = reader.result;
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

/* ============================================================
   MOBILE MENU (the sidebar slides in under 900px)
   ============================================================ */
function toggleNav() {
  const open = !document.body.classList.contains('nav-open');
  document.body.classList.toggle('nav-open', open);
  const b = document.getElementById('nav-toggle');
  if (b) b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
}
function closeNav() {
  document.body.classList.remove('nav-open');
  const b = document.getElementById('nav-toggle');
  if (b) b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
}
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Escape') closeNav(); });
window.addEventListener('resize', () => { if (window.innerWidth > 900) closeNav(); });

function buildNav() {
  const links = [];
  if (currentUser.role === 'CUSTOMER') {
    links.push(['my-tickets', 'My tickets']);
    links.push(['new-ticket', 'New ticket']);
  }
  if (isStaffRole(currentUser.role)) {
    links.push(['queue', 'Ticket queue']);
    links.push(['new-internal-ticket', 'New internal ticket']);
  }
  if (currentUser.role === 'ADMIN') {
    links.push(['admin-users', 'Users']);
    links.push(['admin-tickets', 'Delete / restore tickets']);
    links.push(['admin-categories', 'Categories']);
    links.push(['admin-sla', 'SLA policies']);
    links.push(['admin-reports', 'SLA reports']);
    links.push(['admin-fraud', 'Fraud monitoring']);
    links.push(['admin-events', 'System events']);
    links.push(['admin-audit', 'Audit log']);
  }
  links.push(['notifications', 'Notifications']);
  links.push(['profile', 'My profile']);

  const nav = document.getElementById('nav-links');
  nav.innerHTML = links.map(([key, label]) =>
    `<button class="nav-link" data-view="${key}" onclick="renderView('${key}')">${label}${key === 'notifications' ? '<span id="notif-badge" class="badge hidden">0</span>' : ''}</button>`
  ).join('');
  updateNotifBadge();
}

const notifKey = () => 'bd_notifs_' + (currentUser ? currentUser.id : '');
function loadNotifications() {
  try {
    const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(notifKey()) || '{}');
    notifications = Array.isArray(saved.items) ? saved.items : [];
    unreadCount = saved.unread || 0;
  } catch (e) { notifications = []; unreadCount = 0; }
}
function saveNotifications() {
  try { localStorage.setItem(notifKey(), JSON.stringify({ items: notifications, unread: unreadCount })); } catch (e) { /* storage blocked */ }
}

function updateNotifBadge() {
  const b = document.getElementById('notif-badge');
  if (!b) return;
  b.textContent = unreadCount;
  b.classList.toggle('hidden', unreadCount === 0);
}

function markActiveNav(view) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-link').forEach(b =>
    b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.view === view));
}

function renderView(view, param) {
  closeNav();
  currentView = view;
  currentParam = param ?? null;
  markActiveNav(view.split(':')[0]);
  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
  main.innerHTML = '<p class="empty">Loading…</p>';

  const routes = {
    'my-tickets': renderMyTickets,
    'new-ticket': renderNewTicket,
    'new-internal-ticket': renderNewInternalTicket,
    'queue': renderQueue,
    'ticket-detail': () => renderTicketDetail(param),
    'admin-users': renderAdminUsers,
    'admin-tickets': renderAdminTickets,
    'admin-categories': renderAdminCategories,
    'admin-sla': renderAdminSla,
    'admin-reports': renderAdminReports,
    'admin-fraud': renderAdminFraud,
    'admin-audit': renderAdminAudit,
    'admin-events': renderAdminEvents,
    'notifications': renderNotifications,
    'profile': renderProfile,
  };
  (routes[view] || renderMyTickets)();
}

/* ============================================================
   SHARED BITS
   ============================================================ */
function priorityPill(p) { return `<span class="pill priority-${p}">${p}</span>`; }
function statusPill(s) { return `<span class="pill ${s}">${s.replaceAll('_',' ')}</span>`; }
function fmtDate(d) { return d ? new Date(d).toLocaleString() : '—'; }

const DONE_STATUSES = ['CLOSED','RESOLVED','APPROVED','REJECTED','FALSE_POSITIVE'];

// Same 75% / 90% / breached levels the backend uses for its alerts
function slaInfo(createdAt, dueAt, status) {
  if (!dueAt) return { label: 'No SLA', cls: 'sla-none', pct: 0, bar: '' };
  if (DONE_STATUSES.includes(status)) return { label: 'Closed', cls: 'sla-none', pct: 0, bar: '' };
  const due = new Date(dueAt).getTime();
  const start = createdAt ? new Date(createdAt).getTime() : due - 24 * 36e5;
  const now = Date.now();
  const pct = due > start ? Math.max(0, ((now - start) / (due - start)) * 100) : 100;
  if (now >= due) return { label: 'Breached', cls: 'sla-breach', pct: 100, bar: 'breach' };
  if (pct >= 90) return { label: 'Urgent (90%+)', cls: 'sla-urgent', pct, bar: 'urgent' };
  if (pct >= 75) return { label: 'Warning (75%+)', cls: 'sla-warn', pct, bar: 'warn' };
  return { label: 'On time', cls: 'sla-ok', pct, bar: '' };
}
function slaBadge(t) {
  const info = slaInfo(t.createdAt, t.dueAt, t.status);
  const bar = info.label === 'No SLA' || info.label === 'Closed' ? '' :
    `<div class="sla-bar"><i class="${info.bar}" style="width:${Math.min(100, info.pct).toFixed(0)}%"></i></div>`;
  return `<span class="pill ${info.cls}">${info.label}</span>${bar}`;
}
function firstResponseBadge(t) {
  if (!t.firstResponseDueAt) return '<span class="pill sla-none">—</span>';
  const due = new Date(t.firstResponseDueAt).getTime();
  if (t.firstRespondedAt) {
    const ok = new Date(t.firstRespondedAt).getTime() <= due;
    return `<span class="pill ${ok ? 'sla-ok' : 'sla-breach'}">${ok ? 'Responded in time' : 'Responded late'}</span>`;
  }
  return Date.now() > due ? '<span class="pill sla-breach">Response overdue</span>'
                          : '<span class="pill sla-warn">Awaiting response</span>';
}

function ticketsTable(tickets) {
  if (!tickets.length) return '<p class="empty">No tickets to show.</p>';
  return `
    <table>
      <thead><tr><th>Title</th><th>Category</th><th>Priority</th><th>Status</th><th>SLA</th><th>Assigned to</th><th>Due</th></tr></thead>
      <tbody>
        ${tickets.map(t => `
          <tr class="row-click" onclick="renderView('ticket-detail', ${t.id})">
            <td>${esc(t.title)}${t.source === 'SYSTEM' ? ' <span class="pill ESCALATED">SYSTEM</span>' : ''}</td>
            <td>${esc(t.categoryName)}</td>
            <td>${priorityPill(t.priority)}</td>
            <td>${statusPill(t.status)}</td>
            <td>${slaBadge(t)}</td>
            <td>${esc(t.assignedToEmail) || '—'}</td>
            <td>${fmtDate(t.dueAt)}</td>
          </tr>`).join('')}
      </tbody>
    </table>`;
}

function extractPage(pageResponse) {
  if (!pageResponse) return [];
  return pageResponse.content || [];
}

/* ============================================================
   CUSTOMER: MY TICKETS / NEW TICKET
   ============================================================ */
async function renderMyTickets() {
  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
  try {
    const tickets = await api('/api/tickets/my-tickets');
    main.innerHTML = `
      ${kycBanner()}
      <div class="section-head"><div><h2>My tickets</h2><p>Everything you've raised.</p></div></div>
      ${ticketsTable(tickets)}`;
  } catch (err) { main.innerHTML = `<p class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</p>`; }
}

function kycBanner() {
  if (!currentUser || currentUser.role !== 'CUSTOMER' || currentUser.kycStatus === 'APPROVED') return '';
  return `<div class="banner">Your identity is not verified yet. Until it is, you can only open <b>KYC review</b> tickets.
    <button class="link-btn" onclick="renderView('profile')">Verify now</button></div>`;
}

function renderNewTicket() {
  const visible = categories.filter(c => c.active !== false && c.visibilityToCustomers !== false);
  // same rule as the server: customers that are not verified can only use KYC_REVIEW
  const allowed = currentUser.kycStatus === 'APPROVED' ? visible : visible.filter(c => c.name === 'KYC_REVIEW');
  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
  main.innerHTML = `
    ${kycBanner()}
    <div class="section-head"><div><h2>New ticket</h2><p>Tell us what's going on and we'll route it to the right team.</p></div></div>
    <div class="card ai-card" style="max-width:560px">
      <h3>Not sure what to write?</h3>
      <p class="hint">Describe your problem in your own words and the assistant will prepare the ticket for you. You can edit everything before you submit it.</p>
      <div id="nt-ai-msg"></div>
      <div class="field"><textarea id="nt-ai-text" rows="3" maxlength="4000" placeholder="For example: my card was charged twice yesterday at a shop…"></textarea></div>
      <div class="btn-row"><button class="secondary" id="nt-ai-btn" onclick="aiDraftTicket()">Write it for me</button></div>
      <p class="drop-note">Card numbers, IBANs, emails and phone numbers are hidden before your text reaches the AI. Nothing is sent to us until you press Submit.</p>
      <div id="nt-ai-missing" class="ai-missing"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="card" style="max-width:560px">
      <div id="new-ticket-msg"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>Title</label><input id="nt-title" required></div>
      <div class="field"><label>Description</label><textarea id="nt-desc" rows="4" required></textarea></div>
      <div class="two-col">
        <div class="field"><label>Category</label>
          <select id="nt-category">${allowed.map(c => `<option value="${c.id}">${esc(c.name.replaceAll('_',' '))}</option>`).join('')}</select>
        </div>
        <div class="field"><label>Priority</label>
          <select id="nt-priority">
            <option>LOW</option><option selected>MEDIUM</option><option>HIGH</option><option>CRITICAL</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <button onclick="submitNewTicket()">Submit ticket</button>
    </div>`;
}

async function aiDraftTicket() {
  const text = document.getElementById('nt-ai-text').value.trim();
  const msg = document.getElementById('nt-ai-msg');
  if (!text) { msg.innerHTML = '<div class="msg error">Write a few words about your problem first.</div>'; return; }
  msg.innerHTML = '';
  const btn = document.getElementById('nt-ai-btn');
  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Thinking…';
  try {
    const titleNow = document.getElementById('nt-title').value.trim();
    const d = await api('/api/tickets/ai/draft', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ text, title: titleNow || null }) });
    document.getElementById('nt-title').value = d.title || '';
    document.getElementById('nt-desc').value = d.description || '';
    const cat = document.getElementById('nt-category');
    if (d.categoryId && Array.from(cat.options).some(o => Number(o.value) === d.categoryId)) cat.value = String(d.categoryId);
    const pr = document.getElementById('nt-priority');
    if (d.priority && Array.from(pr.options).some(o => o.value === d.priority)) pr.value = d.priority;
    document.getElementById('nt-ai-missing').innerHTML = (d.missingInfo && d.missingInfo.length)
      ? `<b>Details that would help:</b><ul class="ai-list">${d.missingInfo.map(m => `<li>${esc(m)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>` : '';
    toast('Draft ready. Please read it and edit it before you submit.');
  } catch (err) {
    msg.innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`;
  } finally {
    btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Write it for me';
  }
}

async function submitNewTicket() {
  const title = document.getElementById('nt-title').value;
  const description = document.getElementById('nt-desc').value;
  const categoryId = Number(document.getElementById('nt-category').value);
  const priority = document.getElementById('nt-priority').value;
  const msg = document.getElementById('new-ticket-msg');
  try {
    const ticket = await api('/api/tickets/create', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ title, description, categoryId, priority })
    });
    toast('Ticket #' + ticket.id + ' created.');
    renderView('ticket-detail', ticket.id);
  } catch (err) {
    msg.innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`;
  }
}

async function submitInternalTicket() {

  const description = document.getElementById('it-desc').value;
  const categoryId = Number(document.getElementById('it-category').value);
  const priority = document.getElementById('it-priority').value;
  const customerIdRaw = document.getElementById('it-customer-id').value;
  const customerId = customerIdRaw ? Number(customerIdRaw) : null;
  const msg = document.getElementById('int-ticket-msg');

    const ticket = await api('/api/tickets/create/internal', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ title, description, categoryId, priority, customerId })
    });
    toast('Internal ticket #' + ticket.id + ' created.');
    renderView('ticket-detail', ticket.id);
  } catch (err) {
    msg.innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`;
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   AGENT/ADMIN: QUEUE
   ============================================================ */
async function renderQueue() {
  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
  main.innerHTML = `
    <div class="section-head"><div><h2>Ticket queue</h2><p>Search, filter, and claim work.</p></div></div>
    <div class="card">
      <div class="grid-form">
        <div><label>Status</label><select id="q-status"><option value="">Any</option>
          ${['OPEN','ASSIGNED','IN_PROGRESS','UNDER_REVIEW','ESCALATED','INVESTIGATING','CONFIRMED','FALSE_POSITIVE','APPROVED','REJECTED','RESOLVED','CLOSED']
            .map(s => `<option>${s}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>
        <div><label>Category</label><select id="q-category"><option value="">Any</option>
          ${categories.map(c => `<option value="${c.id}">${esc(c.name.replaceAll('_',' '))}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>
        <div><label>Priority</label><select id="q-priority"><option value="">Any</option>
          <option>LOW</option><option>MEDIUM</option><option>HIGH</option><option>CRITICAL</option></select></div>
        <div class="checkbox-row"><input type="checkbox" id="q-assigned-me"><label style="margin:0">Assigned to me</label></div>
        <div><button onclick="runQueueSearch()">Search</button></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div id="queue-results"><p class="empty">Loading…</p></div>`;
  runQueueSearch();
}

async function runQueueSearch() {
  const status = document.getElementById('q-status').value;
  const categoryId = document.getElementById('q-category').value;
  const priority = document.getElementById('q-priority').value;
  const assignedToMe = document.getElementById('q-assigned-me').checked;
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ page: 0, size: 50 });
  if (status) params.set('status', status);
  if (categoryId) params.set('categoryId', categoryId);
  if (priority) params.set('priority', priority);
  if (assignedToMe) params.set('assignedToId', currentUser.id);

  const results = document.getElementById('queue-results');
  try {
    const page = await api('/api/tickets?' + params.toString());
    results.innerHTML = ticketsTable(extractPage(page));
  } catch (err) { results.innerHTML = `<p class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</p>`; }
}

/* ============================================================
   TICKET DETAIL (shared by customer + staff)
   ============================================================ */
async function renderTicketDetail(ticketId) {
  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
  try {
    const [ticket, comments, history, rawAttachments] = await Promise.all([
      api(`/api/tickets/${ticketId}`),
      api(`/api/tickets/${ticketId}/comments`),
      api(`/api/tickets/${ticketId}/history`),
      api(`/api/tickets/${ticketId}/attachments`).catch(() => getSessionAttachments(ticketId)),
    ]);
    const attachments = normaliseAttachments(rawAttachments);
    window.__attachments = attachments;
    window.__ticket = ticket;

    const isStaff = isStaffRole(currentUser.role);
    const isAdmin = currentUser.role === 'ADMIN';
    const canClaim = currentUser.role === 'AGENT' && !ticket.assignedToEmail && ticket.status === 'OPEN';
    const isClosed = ticket.status === 'CLOSED';

    const statusOptions = ['OPEN','ASSIGNED','IN_PROGRESS','UNDER_REVIEW','ESCALATED','INVESTIGATING',
      'CONFIRMED','FALSE_POSITIVE','APPROVED','REJECTED','RESOLVED','CLOSED'];

    main.innerHTML = `
      <button class="back-link" onclick="renderView(currentUser.role==='CUSTOMER' ? 'my-tickets' : 'queue')">&larr; Back</button>
      <div class="section-head">
        <div>
          <h2>#${ticket.id} &middot; ${esc(ticket.title)}</h2>
          <div class="ticket-meta">
            <span>${statusPill(ticket.status)}</span>
            <span>${priorityPill(ticket.priority)}</span>
            <span>Category: <b>${esc(ticket.categoryName)}</b></span>
            <span>Source: ${esc(ticket.source)}</span>
            <span>Resolution SLA: ${slaBadge(ticket)}</span>
            <span>First response: ${firstResponseBadge(ticket)}</span>
            <span>Due: ${fmtDate(ticket.dueAt)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="card">
        <h3>Description</h3>
        <p style="white-space:pre-wrap">${esc(ticket.description)}</p>
        <hr class="divider">
        <div class="two-col" style="font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-soft)">
          <div>Customer: ${esc(ticket.customerEmail) || '—'}${isStaff && ticket.customerKycStatus ? ` <span class="pill sla-none">KYC: ${esc(ticket.customerKycStatus)}</span>` : ''}</div>
          <div>Created by: ${esc(ticket.createdByEmail) || 'SYSTEM'}</div>
          <div>Assigned to: ${esc(ticket.assignedToEmail) || 'Unassigned'}</div>
          <div>Created: ${fmtDate(ticket.createdAt)}</div>
          <div>First response due: ${fmtDate(ticket.firstResponseDueAt)}</div>
          <div>Resolved: ${fmtDate(ticket.resolvedAt)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      ${isStaff ? `
      <div class="card">
        <h3>Actions</h3>
        <div id="action-msg"></div>
        <div class="btn-row">
          ${canClaim ? `<button class="ok" onclick="doClaim(${ticket.id})">Claim ticket</button>` : ''}
        </div>
        ${!isClosed ? `
          <hr class="divider">
          <div class="grid-form">
            <div><label>Change status to</label>
              <select id="status-select">${statusOptions.map(s => `<option ${s===ticket.status?'selected':''}>${s}</option>`).join('')}</select>
            </div>
            <div><label>Note (required for Escalated / Resolved)</label><input id="status-note"></div>
            <div><button onclick="doChangeStatus(${ticket.id})">Update status</button></div>
          </div>
          <div class="grid-form" style="margin-top:12px">
            <div><label>Escalation note</label><input id="escalate-note"></div>
            <div><button class="secondary" onclick="doEscalate(${ticket.id})">Escalate</button></div>
          </div>
        ` : ''}
        ${isAdmin ? `
          <hr class="divider">
          <div class="grid-form">
            <div><label>Reassign to agent (user ID)</label><input id="reassign-agent" type="number"></div>
            <div><button class="secondary" onclick="doReassign(${ticket.id})">Reassign</button></div>
          </div>
          ${isClosed ? `
          <div class="grid-form" style="margin-top:12px">
            <div><label>Reopen reason</label><input id="reopen-reason"></div>
            <div><button class="secondary" onclick="doReopen(${ticket.id})">Reopen ticket</button></div>
          </div>` : ''}
        ` : ''}
      </div>` : ''}

      ${isStaff ? `
      <div class="card ai-card">
        <h3>AI assistant</h3>
        <p class="hint">Suggestions only. Nothing on the ticket is changed automatically. Card numbers, IBANs, emails and phone numbers are hidden before the text reaches the AI.</p>
        <div id="ai-msg"></div>
        <div class="btn-row">
          <button class="secondary" id="ai-sum-btn" onclick="doAiSummary(${ticket.id})">Summarize ticket</button>
          <button class="secondary" id="ai-tri-btn" onclick="doAiTriage(${ticket.id})">Suggest category &amp; priority</button>
        </div>
        <div id="ai-out-summary">${(aiResults[ticket.id] || {}).summary || ''}</div>
        <div id="ai-out-triage">${(aiResults[ticket.id] || {}).triage || ''}</div>
      </div>` : ''}

      <div class="card">
        <h3>Attachments</h3>
        <div id="attachments-list">
          ${attachments.length ? attachments.map((a, i) => `
            <div class="attachment-row">
              <span>
                ${esc(displayFileName(a.fileName))}
                ${isStaff && a.scanStatus ? ` <span class="pill scan-${esc(a.scanStatus)}" title="${esc(a.scanSummary || '')}">Scan: ${esc(a.scanStatus)}</span>` : ''}
                ${isStaff && a.scanSummary ? `<div class="hint">${esc(a.scanSummary)}</div>` : ''}
              </span>
              <span style="white-space:nowrap">
                ${a.id ? `
                  <button class="link-btn" onclick="downloadAttachment(${i})">Download</button>
                  ${isStaff ? `&nbsp;&middot;&nbsp;<button class="link-btn" onclick="doRescan(${ticket.id}, ${a.id})">Rescan</button>` : ''}
                  &nbsp;&middot;&nbsp;
                  <button class="link-btn" onclick="doDeleteAttachment(${ticket.id}, ${a.id})">Delete</button>
                ` : `<span class="hint">Uploaded (no ID to download by)</span>`}
              </span>
            </div>`).join('') : '<p class="empty">No attachments yet.</p>'}
        </div>
        ${!isClosed ? `
          <hr class="divider">
          <input type="file" id="attachment-files" multiple accept=".png,.jpg,.jpeg,.pdf,image/png,image/jpeg,application/pdf">
          <p class="drop-note">PNG, JPEG or PDF, up to ${MAX_UPLOAD_MB} MB each. ${isStaff ? 'Files are scanned with VirusTotal in the background.' : ''}</p>
          <div class="btn-row"><button id="upload-btn" onclick="doUploadAttachments(${ticket.id})">Upload file(s)</button></div>
        ` : ''}
      </div>

      <div class="card">
        <h3>Comments</h3>
        <div id="comments-list">
          ${comments.length ? comments.map(c => `
            <div class="comment">
              <span class="who">${esc(c.authorEmail)}</span><span class="when">${fmtDate(c.createdAt)}</span>
              <div style="white-space:pre-wrap">${esc(c.commentContent ?? c.content ?? '')}</div>
            </div>`).join('') : '<p class="empty">No comments yet.</p>'}
        </div>
        ${!isClosed ? `
          <hr class="divider">
          <textarea id="new-comment" rows="2" placeholder="Add a comment…"></textarea>
          <div class="btn-row"><button onclick="doAddComment(${ticket.id})">Post comment</button></div>
        ` : ''}
      </div>

      <div class="card">
        <h3>History</h3>
        ${history.length ? history.map(h => `
          <div class="history-row"><b>${esc(h.action)}</b> by ${esc(h.changedByEmail)} — ${fmtDate(h.createdAt)}
            ${h.oldValue || h.newValue ? `(${esc(h.oldValue) || '—'} → ${esc(h.newValue) || '—'})` : ''}</div>
        `).join('') : '<p class="empty">No history yet.</p>'}
      </div>
    `;
  } catch (err) {
    main.innerHTML = `<p class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</p>`;
  }
}

async function doClaim(id) {
  try { await api(`/api/tickets/${id}/claim`, { method: 'PUT' }); toast('Ticket claimed.'); renderView('ticket-detail', id); }
  catch (err) { toast(err.message, true); }
}
async function doChangeStatus(id) {
  const newStatus = document.getElementById('status-select').value;
  const note = document.getElementById('status-note').value;
  try {
    await api(`/api/tickets/${id}/status`, { method: 'PUT', body: JSON.stringify({ newStatus, note }) });
    toast('Status updated.'); renderView('ticket-detail', id);
  } catch (err) { document.getElementById('action-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`; }
}
async function doEscalate(id) {
  const note = document.getElementById('escalate-note').value;
  try {
    await api(`/api/tickets/${id}/escalate`, { method: 'PUT', body: JSON.stringify({ note }) });
    toast('Ticket escalated.'); renderView('ticket-detail', id);
  } catch (err) { document.getElementById('action-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`; }
}
async function doReassign(id) {
  const agentId = Number(document.getElementById('reassign-agent').value);
  try {
    await api(`/api/admin/tickets/${id}/reassign`, { method: 'PUT', body: JSON.stringify({ agentId }) });
    toast('Ticket reassigned.'); renderView('ticket-detail', id);
  } catch (err) { document.getElementById('action-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`; }
}
async function doReopen(id) {
  const reason = document.getElementById('reopen-reason').value;
  try {
    await api(`/api/admin/tickets/${id}/reopen`, { method: 'PUT', body: JSON.stringify({ reason }) });
    toast('Ticket reopened.'); renderView('ticket-detail', id);
  } catch (err) { document.getElementById('action-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`; }
}
async function doAddComment(id) {
  const commentContent = document.getElementById('new-comment').value;
  if (!commentContent.trim()) return;
  try {
    await api(`/api/tickets/${id}/comments`, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ commentContent }) });
    renderView('ticket-detail', id);
  } catch (err) { toast(err.message, true); }
}
function getSessionAttachments(ticketId) {
  try { return JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('bd_attachments_' + ticketId) || '[]'); }
  catch (e) { return []; }
}
function addSessionAttachments(ticketId, fileNames) {
  const updated = getSessionAttachments(ticketId).concat(fileNames.map(fileName => ({ fileName })));
  try { sessionStorage.setItem('bd_attachments_' + ticketId, JSON.stringify(updated)); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
}
function normaliseAttachments(list) {
  if (!Array.isArray(list)) return [];
  return list.map(a => typeof a === 'string' ? { fileName: a } : a);
}
function displayFileName(name) {
  return String(name || '').replace(/^[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}_/, '');
}
function validateFiles(fileList) {
  for (const f of fileList) {
    if (!ALLOWED_UPLOAD_TYPES.includes(f.type)) return `"${f.name}" is not allowed. Only PNG, JPEG and PDF files can be uploaded.`;
    if (f.size === 0) return `"${f.name}" is empty.`;
    if (f.size > MAX_UPLOAD_MB * 1024 * 1024) return `"${f.name}" is larger than ${MAX_UPLOAD_MB} MB.`;
  }
  return null;
}

async function doUploadAttachments(id) {
  const input = document.getElementById('attachment-files');
  if (!input.files.length) { toast('Choose at least one file first', true); return; }
  const problem = validateFiles(input.files);
  if (problem) { toast(problem, true); return; }

  const formData = new FormData();
  for (const file of input.files) formData.append('file', file);
  const btn = document.getElementById('upload-btn');
  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Uploading…';
  try {
    const savedFileNames = await api(`/api/tickets/${id}/attachments`, { method: 'POST', body: formData });
    addSessionAttachments(id, Array.isArray(savedFileNames) ? savedFileNames : []);
    toast(input.files.length + ' file(s) uploaded.');
    renderView('ticket-detail', id);
  } catch (err) {
    toast(err.message, true);
    btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Upload file(s)';
  }
}
function downloadAttachment(index) {
  const a = (window.__attachments || [])[index];
  if (a && a.id) downloadFile(`/api/tickets/attachments/${a.id}`, displayFileName(a.fileName));
}
async function doDeleteAttachment(ticketId, attachmentId) {
  if (!confirm('Delete this attachment?')) return;
  try {
    await api(`/api/tickets/attachments/${attachmentId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    toast('Attachment deleted.');
    renderView('ticket-detail', ticketId);
  } catch (err) { toast(err.message, true); }
}
async function doRescan(ticketId, attachmentId) {
  try {
    await api(`/api/tickets/attachments/${attachmentId}/rescan`, { method: 'POST' });
    toast('Rescan queued. It can take a minute because VirusTotal limits free requests.');
    setTimeout(() => { if (currentView === 'ticket-detail' && currentParam === ticketId) renderView('ticket-detail', ticketId); }, 20000);
  } catch (err) { toast(err.message, true); }
}

/* ============================================================
   CUSTOMER: IDENTITY VERIFICATION (KYC)  (/api/kyc/status, /api/kyc/start)
   ============================================================ */
const KYC_TEXT = {
  NOT_STARTED: ['Not verified', 'Verify your identity to open tickets in every category.'],
  PENDING: ['Verification started', 'Finish the verification steps. If you already did, it can take a moment to update.'],
  IN_REVIEW: ['Under review', 'Your documents are being reviewed. We will email you when it is done.'],
  APPROVED: ['Verified', 'Your identity is verified. You can open tickets in every category.'],
  DECLINED: ['Declined', 'Your verification was declined. You can try again, or open a KYC review ticket for help.'],
  ABANDONED: ['Not finished', 'You did not finish the verification. You can start again.'],
  EXPIRED: ['Expired', 'Your verification session expired. Please start again.'],
};
async function loadKycCard() {
  const box = document.getElementById('kyc-card');
  if (!box) return;
  try {
    const st = await api('/api/kyc/status');
    currentUser.kycStatus = st.status;
    const [label, text] = KYC_TEXT[st.status] || [st.status, ''];
    const canStart = st.status !== 'APPROVED' && st.status !== 'IN_REVIEW';
    const box2 = document.getElementById('kyc-card');
    if (!box2) return;                       // the user left the profile page meanwhile
    box2.innerHTML = `
      <h3>Identity verification</h3>
      <div id="kyc-msg"></div>
      <p>Status: <span class="pill ${st.status === 'APPROVED' ? 'sla-ok' : (st.status === 'DECLINED' || st.status === 'EXPIRED' ? 'sla-breach' : 'sla-warn')}">${esc(label)}</span></p>
      <p>${esc(text)}</p>
      ${st.verifiedAt ? `<p class="hint">Verified on ${fmtDate(st.verifiedAt)}</p>` : ''}
      <div class="btn-row">
        ${canStart ? `<button id="kyc-btn" onclick="doStartKyc()">${st.status === 'NOT_STARTED' ? 'Verify now' : 'Verify again'}</button>` : ''}
        <button class="secondary" onclick="loadKycCard()">Refresh status</button>
      </div>
      ${canStart ? '<p class="drop-note">You will be taken to our verification partner and brought back here when you finish.</p>' : ''}`;
  } catch (err) { box.innerHTML = `<h3>Identity verification</h3><p class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</p>`; }
}
async function doStartKyc() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('kyc-btn');
  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Starting…';
  try {
    const r = await api('/api/kyc/start', { method: 'POST' });
    window.location.href = r.verificationUrl;
  } catch (err) {
    btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Verify now';
    document.getElementById('kyc-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`;
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   AGENT/ADMIN: AI ASSISTANT  (/api/tickets/{id}/ai/summary, /ai/triage)
   ============================================================ */
async function doAiSummary(id) {
  await runAi('ai-sum-btn', 'Summarizing…', async () => {
    const r = await api(`/api/tickets/${id}/ai/summary`, { method: 'POST' });
    const list = items => items && items.length ? `<ul class="ai-list">${items.map(i => `<li>${esc(i)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>` : null;
    const html = `<div class="ai-out"><h4>Summary</h4><p style="white-space:pre-wrap">${esc(r.summary)}</p>
      ${list(r.keyPoints) ? `<h4>Key points</h4>${list(r.keyPoints)}` : ''}
      <h4>Still open</h4>${list(r.openItems) || '<p class="hint">Nothing outstanding.</p>'}</div>`;
    (aiResults[id] = aiResults[id] || {}).summary = html;
    const box = document.getElementById('ai-out-summary'); if (box) box.innerHTML = html;
  });
}
async function doAiTriage(id) {
  await runAi('ai-tri-btn', 'Thinking…', async () => {
    const r = await api(`/api/tickets/${id}/ai/triage`, { method: 'POST' });
    const cur = window.__ticket || {};
    const catName = String(r.categoryName || '').replaceAll('_', ' ');
    const sameCat = cur.categoryName && cur.categoryName === r.categoryName;
    const samePri = cur.priority && cur.priority === r.priority;
    const html = `<div class="ai-out"><h4>Suggested routing</h4>
      <p>Category: <b>${esc(catName)}</b> <span class="hint">${sameCat ? '(same as now)' : '(now: ' + esc(String(cur.categoryName || '—').replaceAll('_', ' ')) + ')'}</span><br>
      Priority: ${priorityPill(r.priority)} <span class="hint">${samePri ? '(same as now)' : '(now: ' + esc(cur.priority || '—') + ')'}</span><br>
      Confidence: <span class="pill sla-none">${esc(r.confidence)}</span></p>
      <p class="hint">${esc(r.reason)}</p>
      <p class="hint">Suggestion only. Nothing was changed.</p></div>`;
    (aiResults[id] = aiResults[id] || {}).triage = html;
    const box = document.getElementById('ai-out-triage'); if (box) box.innerHTML = html;
  });
}
async function runAi(btnId, busyLabel, work) {
  const btn = document.getElementById(btnId);
  const old = btn.textContent;
  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = busyLabel;
  const msg = document.getElementById('ai-msg'); if (msg) msg.innerHTML = '';
  try { await work(); }
  catch (err) { const m = document.getElementById('ai-msg'); if (m) m.innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`; }
  finally { if (btn.isConnected) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = old; } }
}

/* ============================================================
   ADMIN: SYSTEM EVENTS (Kafka)
   Needs the backend event monitor: GET /api/admin/system-events plus live
   pushes on the SSE stream shaped { kind: 'system-event', timestamp, topic, key, summary, result }
   ============================================================ */
const KAFKA_TOPICS = [
  ['email-events', 'ticket-service', 'notification-service', 'Emails to send (ticket created, status changed, SLA alerts and reports)'],
  ['live-events', 'ticket-service', 'notification-service', 'Live pop-up notifications pushed to the browser (SSE)'],
  ['fraud-events', 'fraud-service', 'ticket-service', 'A fraud or AML rule matched, so a ticket must be opened'],
  ['ticket-events', 'ticket-service', 'fraud-service', 'The ticket that was opened, so the alert can be linked to it'],
  ['kyc-events', 'identity-service', 'ticket-service', 'Identity verification finished (approved, declined, ...)'],
  ['<topic>.DLT', 'any consumer', 'nobody (kept for inspection)', 'Messages that failed after 5 retries are parked here'],
];
async function renderAdminEvents() {
  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
  main.innerHTML = `
    <div class="section-head"><div><h2>System events</h2>
      <p>The messages the services send each other through Kafka. New events appear here live.</p></div>
      <button class="secondary" onclick="renderAdminEvents()">Refresh</button></div>
    <div id="events-live"><p class="empty">Loading…</p></div>
    <div class="section-head" style="margin-top:22px"><div><h3>Topics</h3></div></div>
    <table>
      <thead><tr><th>Topic</th><th>From</th><th>To</th><th>What it carries</th></tr></thead>
      <tbody>${KAFKA_TOPICS.map(t => `<tr><td><b>${esc(t[0])}</b></td><td>${esc(t[1])}</td><td>${esc(t[2])}</td><td>${esc(t[3])}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody>
    </table>`;
  try {
    const list = await api('/api/admin/system-events');
    systemEvents = Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];
    drawSystemEvents();
  } catch (err) {
    document.getElementById('events-live').innerHTML =
      `<div class="banner">The live event list is not available on this server yet. The topics below are still accurate.
        <div class="hint">(${esc(err.message)})</div></div>`;
  }
}
function addSystemEvent(e) {
  systemEvents.unshift(e);
  systemEvents = systemEvents.slice(0, 200);
  if (currentView === 'admin-events' && document.getElementById('events-body')) drawSystemEvents();
}
function drawSystemEvents() {
  const host = document.getElementById('events-live');
  if (!host) return;
  if (!systemEvents.length) { host.innerHTML = '<p class="empty">No events yet. Create a ticket or run a fraud scenario and watch this page.</p>'; return; }
  host.innerHTML = `
    <table>
      <thead><tr><th>When</th><th>Topic</th><th>Key</th><th>What happened</th><th>Result</th></tr></thead>
      <tbody id="events-body">${systemEvents.map(e => {
        const bad = e.result && e.result !== 'OK';
        return `<tr><td>${fmtDate(e.timestamp)}</td><td><span class="pill sla-none">${esc(e.topic)}</span></td><td>${esc(e.key) || '—'}</td>
          <td>${esc(e.summary)}</td><td><span class="pill ${bad ? 'ESCALATED' : 'RESOLVED'}">${esc(e.result || 'OK')}</span></td></tr>`;
      }).join('')}</tbody>
    </table>`;
}

/* ============================================================
   ADMIN: USERS
   ============================================================ */
async function renderAdminUsers() {
  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
  try {
    const users = await api('/api/admin/users');
    main.innerHTML = `
      <div class="section-head"><div><h2>Users</h2><p>Manage roles and account status.</p></div></div>
      <table>
        <thead><tr><th>ID</th><th>Email</th><th>Name</th><th>Role</th><th>Status</th><th>Verified</th><th>KYC</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
          ${users.map(u => `
            <tr>
              <td>${u.id}</td><td>${esc(u.email)}</td><td>${esc(u.fullName)}</td>
              <td>
                <select onchange="doChangeRole(${u.id}, this.value)">
                  ${['CUSTOMER','AGENT','ADMIN'].map(r => `<option ${r===u.role?'selected':''}>${r}</option>`).join('')}
                </select>
              </td>
              <td>${u.status}</td>
              <td>${u.emailVerified ? 'Yes' : 'No'}</td>
              <td>${esc(u.kycStatus || '—')}</td>
              <td>
                ${u.status === 'ACTIVE'
                  ? `<button class="danger" onclick="doDeactivate(${u.id})">Deactivate</button>`
                  : `<button class="ok" onclick="doReactivate(${u.id})">Reactivate</button>`}
              </td>
            </tr>`).join('')}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <div class="card" style="margin-top:22px;max-width:500px">
        <h3>Assign categories to an agent</h3>
        <p style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink-soft)">Use the ID column above to find an agent's user ID.</p>
        <div class="field"><label>Agent user ID</label><input id="assign-agent-id" type="number"></div>
        <div class="field"><label>Categories</label>
          <select id="assign-category-ids" multiple size="7">
            ${categories.map(c => `<option value="${c.id}">${esc(c.name.replaceAll('_',' '))}</option>`).join('')}
          </select>
        </div>
        <button onclick="doAssignCategories()">Assign</button>
      </div>`;
  } catch (err) { main.innerHTML = `<p class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</p>`; }
}
async function doChangeRole(userId, newRole) {
  try { await api(`/api/admin/users/${userId}/role`, { method: 'PUT', body: JSON.stringify({ newRole }) }); toast('Role updated.'); }
  catch (err) { toast(err.message, true); renderAdminUsers(); }
}
async function doDeactivate(userId) {
  try { await api(`/api/admin/users/${userId}/deactivate`, { method: 'PUT' }); toast('User deactivated.'); renderAdminUsers(); }
  catch (err) { toast(err.message, true); }
}
async function doReactivate(userId) {
  try { await api(`/api/admin/users/${userId}/reactivate`, { method: 'PUT' }); toast('User reactivated.'); renderAdminUsers(); }
  catch (err) { toast(err.message, true); }
}
async function doAssignCategories() {
  const agentId = Number(document.getElementById('assign-agent-id').value);
  const categoryIds = Array.from(document.getElementById('assign-category-ids').selectedOptions).map(o => Number(o.value));
  try {
    await api(`/api/admin/users/${agentId}/categories`, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ categoryIds }) });
    toast('Categories assigned.');
  } catch (err) { toast(err.message, true); }
}

/* ============================================================
   ADMIN: DELETE / RESTORE TICKETS (soft delete)
   active tickets  GET /api/tickets
   deleted tickets GET /api/admin/tickets/deleted
   soft delete     PUT /api/admin/tickets/{id}/soft-delete
   restore         PUT /api/admin/tickets/{id}/restore
   ============================================================ */
async function renderAdminTickets() {
  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
  try {
    const [activePage, deleted] = await Promise.all([
      api('/api/tickets?' + new URLSearchParams({ page: 0, size: 100 }).toString()),
      api('/api/admin/tickets/deleted')
    ]);
    const active = extractPage(activePage);
    const activeOption = t => `<option value="${t.id}">#${t.id} · ${esc(t.title)} (${esc(String(t.status).replaceAll('_', ' '))})</option>`;
    const deletedOption = t => `<option value="${t.id}">#${t.id} · ${esc(t.title)} (deleted ${esc(fmtDate(t.deletedAt))})</option>`;
    main.innerHTML = `
      <div class="section-head"><div><h2>Delete / restore tickets</h2>
        <p>A deleted ticket is hidden from customers and agents but stays in the database, so it can be restored.</p></div></div>
      <div class="soft-grid">
        <div class="card">
          <h3>Soft delete a ticket</h3>
          <div class="field"><label>Pick a ticket</label>
            <select id="sd-pick"><option value="">Select a ticket…</option>${active.map(activeOption).join('')}</select></div>
          <div class="field"><label>…or type a ticket ID</label>
            <input id="sd-id" type="number" min="1" inputmode="numeric" placeholder="e.g. 8"></div>
          <button class="danger" onclick="doSoftDelete()">Soft delete</button>
          ${active.length >= 100 ? '<p class="drop-note">Showing the first 100 tickets. Type an ID for any other ticket.</p>' : ''}
        </div>
        <div class="card">
          <h3>Restore a ticket <span class="pill deleted">${deleted.length} deleted</span></h3>
          ${deleted.length ? `
            <div class="field"><label>Deleted tickets</label>
              <select id="rs-pick"><option value="">Select a ticket…</option>${deleted.map(deletedOption).join('')}</select></div>
            <button class="ok" onclick="doRestore()">Restore</button>`
          : '<p class="empty">No deleted tickets.</p>'}
        </div>
      </div>`;
  } catch (err) { main.innerHTML = `<p class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</p>`; }
}
async function doSoftDelete() {
  const typed = document.getElementById('sd-id').value.trim();
  const id = Number(typed || document.getElementById('sd-pick').value);
  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id < 1) { toast('Pick a ticket or type a ticket ID.', true); return; }
  if (!confirm('Soft delete ticket #' + id + '? It will be hidden from everyone until you restore it.')) return;
  try {
    await api(`/api/admin/tickets/${id}/soft-delete`, { method: 'PUT' });
    toast('Ticket #' + id + ' deleted.');
    renderAdminTickets();
  } catch (err) { toast(err.message, true); }
}
async function doRestore() {
  const id = Number(document.getElementById('rs-pick').value);
  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id < 1) { toast('Pick a ticket to restore.', true); return; }
  try {
    await api(`/api/admin/tickets/${id}/restore`, { method: 'PUT' });
    toast('Ticket #' + id + ' restored.');
    renderAdminTickets();
  } catch (err) { toast(err.message, true); }
}

/* ============================================================
   ADMIN: CATEGORIES
   ============================================================ */
async function renderAdminCategories() {
  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
  try {
    const all = await api('/api/categories');
    main.innerHTML = `
      <div class="section-head"><div><h2>Categories</h2><p>Control visibility to customers.</p></div></div>
      <table>
        <thead><tr><th>Name</th><th>Visible to customers</th><th>Active</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
          ${all.map(c => `
            <tr>
              <td>${esc(c.name)}</td>
              <td>${c.visibilityToCustomers ? 'Yes' : 'No'}</td>
              <td>${c.active ? 'Yes' : 'No'}</td>
              <td><button class="secondary" onclick="doToggleCategoryVisibility(${c.id}, ${!c.visibilityToCustomers}, '${esc(c.name)}')">
                ${c.visibilityToCustomers ? 'Hide from customers' : 'Show to customers'}
              </button></td>
            </tr>`).join('')}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <div class="card" style="margin-top:22px;max-width:460px">
        <h3>New category</h3>
        <div class="field"><label>Name</label><input id="new-cat-name"></div>
        <div class="checkbox-row field"><input type="checkbox" id="new-cat-visible"><label style="margin:0">Visible to customers</label></div>
        <button onclick="doCreateCategory()">Create</button>
      </div>`;
  } catch (err) { main.innerHTML = `<p class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</p>`; }
}
async function doToggleCategoryVisibility(id, newValue, name) {
  try {
    await api(`/api/admin/categories/${id}`, { method: 'PUT', body: JSON.stringify({ name, visibilityToCustomers: newValue }) });
    toast('Category updated.'); renderAdminCategories();
  } catch (err) { toast(err.message, true); }
}
async function doCreateCategory() {
  const name = document.getElementById('new-cat-name').value;
  const visibilityToCustomers = document.getElementById('new-cat-visible').checked;
  try {
    await api('/api/admin/categories', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ name, visibilityToCustomers }) });
    toast('Category created.');
    categories = await api('/api/categories');
    renderAdminCategories();
  } catch (err) { toast(err.message, true); }
}

/* ============================================================
   ADMIN: SLA POLICIES  (GET/PUT /api/admin/sla-policies)
   ============================================================ */
async function renderAdminSla() {
  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
  try {
    const policies = await api('/api/admin/sla-policies');
    const order = { CRITICAL: 0, HIGH: 1, MEDIUM: 2, LOW: 3 };
    policies.sort((a, b) => (a.category?.name || '').localeCompare(b.category?.name || '') || order[a.priority] - order[b.priority]);
    main.innerHTML = `
      <div class="section-head"><div><h2>SLA policies</h2>
        <p>How long each category and priority has for a first response and for resolution. Changes apply to tickets created from now on.</p></div></div>
      <div id="sla-msg"></div>
      <table>
        <thead><tr><th>Category</th><th>Priority</th><th>First response (min)</th><th>Resolution (min)</th><th></th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
          ${policies.map(po => `
            <tr>
              <td>${esc((po.category?.name || '').replaceAll('_', ' '))}</td>
              <td>${priorityPill(po.priority)}</td>
              <td><input class="inline" type="number" min="1" id="sla-fr-${po.id}" value="${po.firstResponseMinutes}"></td>
              <td><input class="inline" type="number" min="1" id="sla-res-${po.id}" value="${po.resolutionMinutes}"></td>
              <td><button class="secondary" onclick="doSaveSlaPolicy(${po.id})">Save</button></td>
            </tr>`).join('')}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      ${!policies.length ? '<p class="empty">No SLA policies found.</p>' : ''}`;
  } catch (err) { main.innerHTML = `<p class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</p>`; }
}
async function doSaveSlaPolicy(id) {
  const firstResponseMinutes = Number(document.getElementById('sla-fr-' + id).value);
  const resolutionMinutes = Number(document.getElementById('sla-res-' + id).value);
  if (!(firstResponseMinutes >= 1) || !(resolutionMinutes >= 1)) { toast('Both values must be at least 1 minute', true); return; }
  try {
    await api('/api/admin/sla-policies/' + id, { method: 'PUT', body: JSON.stringify({ firstResponseMinutes, resolutionMinutes }) });
    toast('SLA policy saved.');
  } catch (err) { toast(err.message, true); }
}

/* ============================================================
   ADMIN: SLA REPORTS  (/api/admin/sla-reports)
   ============================================================ */
async function renderAdminReports() {
  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
  try {
    const reports = await api('/api/admin/sla-reports');
    const d = new Date(); d.setMonth(d.getMonth() - 1);
    const lastMonth = d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    main.innerHTML = `
      <div class="section-head"><div><h2>SLA reports</h2>
        <p>A report is created automatically each month. You can also generate one yourself; each month has only one report.</p></div>
        <button class="secondary" onclick="downloadFile('/api/admin/sla-reports/latest/download', 'sla-report-latest.csv')">Download latest CSV</button>
      </div>
      <div class="card" style="max-width:420px">
        <h3>Generate a report</h3>
        <div id="report-msg"></div>
        <div class="field"><label>Month</label><input type="month" id="report-month" value="${lastMonth}"></div>
        <button onclick="doGenerateReport()">Generate</button>
      </div>
      <table>
        <thead><tr><th>Period</th><th>Status</th><th>Tickets</th><th>Resolution compliance</th><th>Generated</th><th>By</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
          ${reports.map(r => `
            <tr>
              <td><b>${esc(r.period)}</b></td>
              <td><span class="pill ${r.status === 'FAILED' ? 'ESCALATED' : 'RESOLVED'}">${esc(r.status)}</span></td>
              <td>${r.totalTickets ?? '—'}</td>
              <td>${r.resolutionCompliancePercent != null ? Number(r.resolutionCompliancePercent).toFixed(1) + '%' : '—'}</td>
              <td>${fmtDate(r.generatedAt)}</td>
              <td>${esc(r.generatedBy)}</td>
            </tr>`).join('')}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      ${!reports.length ? '<p class="empty">No reports yet.</p>' : ''}`;
  } catch (err) { main.innerHTML = `<p class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</p>`; }
}
async function doGenerateReport() {
  const month = document.getElementById('report-month').value;
  if (!month) { document.getElementById('report-msg').innerHTML = '<div class="msg error">Choose a month.</div>'; return; }
  try {
    await api('/api/admin/sla-reports?month=' + encodeURIComponent(month), { method: 'POST' });
    toast('Report for ' + month + ' generated.');
    renderAdminReports();
  } catch (err) { document.getElementById('report-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`; }
}

/* ============================================================
   ADMIN: FRAUD MONITORING  (/api/admin/transactions, /transactions/simulate, /fraud-alerts)
   ============================================================ */
const SCENARIOS = {
  LARGE: 'Large transfer',
  STRUCTURING: 'Structuring (deposits just under the limit)',
  VELOCITY: 'Rapid activity (many transactions fast)',
  RISKY_COUNTRY: 'High-risk country',
  GREY_LIST_COUNTRY: 'Grey-list country',
};
async function renderAdminFraud() {
  const main = document.getElementById('main-content');
  try {
    const [alerts, users] = await Promise.all([api('/api/admin/fraud-alerts'), api('/api/admin/users')]);
    const customers = users.filter(u => u.role === 'CUSTOMER');
    const customerOptions = customers.map(u => `<option value="${u.id}">${esc(u.fullName)} (${esc(u.email)})</option>`).join('');
    main.innerHTML = `
      <div class="section-head"><div><h2>Fraud monitoring</h2>
        <p>Transactions are checked against the fraud and AML rules. A match creates an internal ticket for the right team. The customer is not told.</p></div></div>
      <div class="two-col">
        <div class="card">
          <h3>Simulate a suspicious pattern</h3>
          <div id="sim-msg"></div>
          <div class="field"><label>Customer</label><select id="sim-customer">${customerOptions}</select></div>
          <div class="field"><label>Scenario</label>
            <select id="sim-scenario">${Object.entries(SCENARIOS).map(([k, v]) => `<option value="${k}">${v}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>
          <button onclick="doSimulate()">Run scenario</button>
        </div>
        <div class="card">
          <h3>Record one transaction</h3>
          <div id="tx-msg"></div>
          <div class="field"><label>Customer</label><select id="tx-customer">${customerOptions}</select></div>
          <div class="two-col">
            <div class="field"><label>Account reference</label><input id="tx-account" value="ACC-1001"></div>
            <div class="field"><label>Type</label><select id="tx-type">
              ${['CASH_DEPOSIT', 'CASH_WITHDRAWAL', 'TRANSFER', 'CARD_PAYMENT'].map(t => `<option>${t}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>
            <div class="field"><label>Amount</label><input id="tx-amount" type="number" min="0.01" step="0.01" value="500"></div>
            <div class="field"><label>Currency</label><input id="tx-currency" maxlength="3" value="BHD"></div>
            <div class="field"><label>Country (2 letters)</label><input id="tx-country" maxlength="2" value="BH"></div>
          </div>
          <button onclick="doRecordTransaction()">Record</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="section-head" style="margin-top:8px"><div><h3>Fraud alerts</h3></div>
        <button class="secondary" onclick="renderAdminFraud()">Refresh</button></div>
      <table>
        <thead><tr><th>When</th><th>Rule</th><th>Customer ID</th><th>Transaction</th><th>Ticket</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
          ${alerts.map(a => `
            <tr>
              <td>${fmtDate(a.createdAt)}</td>
              <td><b>${esc(a.ruleCode)}</b></td>
              <td>${a.customerId}</td>
              <td>${a.transactionId ?? '—'}</td>
              <td>${a.ticketId ? `<button class="link-btn" onclick="renderView('ticket-detail', ${a.ticketId})">Open #${a.ticketId}</button>` : '—'}</td>
            </tr>`).join('')}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      ${!alerts.length ? '<p class="empty">No alerts yet. Run a scenario above.</p>' : ''}`;
  } catch (err) { main.innerHTML = `<p class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</p>`; }
}
async function doSimulate() {
  const customerId = Number(document.getElementById('sim-customer').value);
  const scenario = document.getElementById('sim-scenario').value;
  try {
    const txs = await api('/api/admin/transactions/simulate', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ customerId, scenario }) });
    toast(txs.length + ' transaction(s) recorded. Alerts appear in a moment.');
    setTimeout(() => { if (currentView === 'admin-fraud') renderAdminFraud(); }, 1500);
  } catch (err) { document.getElementById('sim-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`; }
}
async function doRecordTransaction() {
  const body = {
    customerId: Number(document.getElementById('tx-customer').value),
    accountRef: document.getElementById('tx-account').value,
    type: document.getElementById('tx-type').value,
    amount: Number(document.getElementById('tx-amount').value),
    currency: document.getElementById('tx-currency').value.toUpperCase(),
    country: document.getElementById('tx-country').value.toUpperCase(),
  };
  try {
    await api('/api/admin/transactions', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) });
    toast('Transaction recorded.');
    setTimeout(() => { if (currentView === 'admin-fraud') renderAdminFraud(); }, 1500);
  } catch (err) { document.getElementById('tx-msg').innerHTML = `<div class="msg error">${esc(err.message)}</div>`; }
}
